# Interface changes and confirmations: digital / clock owner

## R1 precondition (consolidation-pass-2.md): host_follow with on-chip half/quarter CDR

**Confirmed: no selected wired rate needs `clocktree.config.wire_host_source=host_follow`
while the on-chip half/quarter-rate CDR runs.** From the digital side, R1 (three
`pll_ring` instances become two) may proceed.

Basis:

- In wired modes the transport host word clock is `line_divided`, the register default.
  It is the TX line clock divided by `wire_host_divider` (4..32). The host link is
  defined as FPGA-follows-chip: the FPGA derives H2D timing from the forwarded D2H
  clock (rtl/pt_host_link.sv, "synchronous profile"). So no wired rate needs the
  chip's host clock to lock to the FPGA's H2D clock.
- ~~Divider 10 gives full throughput~~ **Corrected (S1):** dense framing carries
  59/64 payload words, so divider 10 gives only 0.922 × line rate. The rule is
  divider 8 (host word clock = line/8, 1.152 × line framed capacity). This still
  comes from `line_divided`: 312.5 MHz at 2.5 Gb/s, which is the top of the host
  pad budget. 2.97 Gb/s (371 MHz) does not fit and stays non-gating. The
  conclusion (no host_follow needed) is unchanged, because divider 8 is also
  `line_divided`.
- The CDR (half/quarter, Ring C) is receive-only. RX data enters the macro through
  the `wire_rx_clk` → host async FIFO (`wire_in`). That crossing absorbs the RX/TX
  ppm offset through the dense link's per-frame payload counts, so the host clock
  never has to come from the RX ring.
- `host_follow` stays required only for RF/passive-LO modes (P3-3), where Ring C is
  not tracking. This matches R1's Ring C owner rule.

Caveat (not an R1 blocker): full-rate 312.5 MHz host timing is not closed yet (see
path-to-tapeout.md section 1). The new reduced-rate fallback `debug.host_link.rate`
(/2, /4) runs the transport from the same `line_divided` clock, so it does not
introduce a host_follow dependency either.

## New register field: `debug.host_link.rate` (2 bits, stopped-only, reset `full`)

Values: `full`, `div2`, `div4`. These set the host link word side and the whole
transport core to host word clock /1, /2 or /4. Implemented in
`pt_host_rate_clock` (rtl/pt_host_link.sv). A change holds the host link and core in
reset while the clock select switches. The wired/RF line rates must be lowered
to match through the clocktree dividers. ABI: debug_cfg grows from 241 to 243
bits, the map hash changes (0xC0E4 at the time of writing), and the macro port
list is unchanged.

# Interface changes: spec owner (host-link capacity, on-chip CDR, power) — 2026-11-09

Full spec: `projects/programmable_transceiver_platform/spec/interface-architecture-changes.md` (AC-1..AC-4).
Physical-limit check: `scratch/reviews/physical-limits.md` (no item in this scope is shown to be a physical limit; L1/L2 device-speed items are model-based estimates pending extracted simulation).

| # | Change | Owner(s) | Concrete ask |
|---|---|---|---|
| S1 | **Divider rule 10 → 8** for every framed wired rate (host word clock = line/8; 312.5 MHz at 2.5 Gb/s). Divider 10 gives 0.922 × line rate framed capacity: every wired rate is 7.8 % short. | digital/clock | Change `wire_host_divider` default/recipes; correct the R1 note above ("divider 10 … full throughput" is wrong with 59/64 dense framing). |
| S2 | **Core gearbox**: pad-edge DDR + 1:4/4:1 at 156.25 MHz in two edge regions (D2H south, H2D north); transport core at word/4 with 40-bit words (78.125 MHz at PCIe). | digital | Re-floorplan + re-harden on current RTL; pipeline FIFO read muxes, framer, regfile commit; in-envelope STA (22/32 °C × 3.15/3.35 V Liberty or derated tt). |
| S3 | **Wired boundary 20 b @ line/20 → 40 b @ line/40** (62.5 MHz at 2.5 Gb/s). | digital + wired_rx + wired_tx + integration | +20 RX and +20 TX macro data ports; one extra 2:1 deserializer stage in wired_rx; one extra 2:1 serializer stage in wired_tx; integration netlist port update. |
| S4 | **CDR: per-UI Alexander PD** (data+edge samplers, 8 at quarter rate / 4 at half) voting per ring cycle straight into the BB pump; continuous integral path (no HOLD hand-over), range +600…−5600 ppm, slew ≥400 ppm/µs; BW 0.5–4 MHz programmable, peaking ≤2 dB; RTL keeps FLL/lock/status only. | wired_rx (cdr, wrx_cap, slicer), digital (`pt_wrx_cdr_loop` reduced to FLL/status) | Samplers resolve ≤0.35 ring period at ss/32 °C/3.15 V (625 and 810 MHz); sampler offset ≤10 mV after trim (≤2 mV step). |
| S5 | **Ring phase trim**: 8 phases at quarter rate, spacing error ≤3° after per-phase delay trim (≥5 bit, ≤1° step), calibrated from edge-sampler histograms. Ring RJ ≤2 ps rms. | timing (pll_ring) + digital (trim registers, histogram counters) | New trim DAC/delay per phase; new register fields `cdr.phase_trim[7:0][4:0]`, histogram readout. |
| S6 | **HDMI/DVI RX on-chip**: forwarded TMDS clock into `WCLK_IN` as CDR reference, ring ×10, phase-only tracking. | timing (clockmux REF select from WCLK_IN), wired_rx | Clock select path WCLK_IN → ring REF (≤150 MHz). |
| S7 | **Wired pad loading ≤1.6 pF effective at 1.25 GHz** (RL ≥10 dB), via low-C protection variant or T-coil (5.9 nH/leg). | pads (+ wired_rx/wired_tx for device C) | Today RX 2.35 pF, TX 2.71 pF. |
| S8 | **Wired TX swing ≥800 mVppd** for PCIe (CML tail ≥16 mA, 20 mA preferred, inside 36 mA wiredtx allocation). | wired_tx | Today 578.5 mVppd at tt. |
| S9 | **Digital power**: ≤60 mA wired/USB, ≤40 mA RF (unused domains clock-gated); vector-based (SAIF) power from dense-service sims replaces the vectorless 207 mA. | digital | Without this the mode ceilings fail by up to 135 mA (spec/budgets/power-budget.json). |
| S10 | **RF modes run the host link at div2** (`debug.host_link.rate`), halving host pin current. | digital (recipe), host FPGA | 1.44 Gb/s framed ≥ 0.96 Gb/s at 40 MS/s. |

Board CDR/retimer/serializer routes (board-requirements.md B13–B16) are recorded as **backup** entries only.

## Macro outline and port list (pt_digital_macro) — settled for S2/S3

- Outline: unchanged at **1580 × 1580 µm (2.496 mm²)**. Working LEF: `scratch/digital-outline/pt_digital_macro.lef`.
- Pin edges (`pin_order.cfg`):
  - South: host word clock, reset/POR, D2H, SPI.
  - North: H2D, `debug_cfg`.
  - West: wired/RF data and clocks, frequency-counter sources, clocktree slow I/O.
  - East: `analog_cfg`, island/ATB/observation.

| `wire_tx_data` | [19:0] | **[39:0]**, same ordering, launched for the serializer's falling-edge load |
| `wire_rx_valid`, `wire_tx_valid`, `wire_tx_request`, `wire_idle` | per double word | per quad word |
| `host_word_clk` | 312.5 MHz word clock, core directly on it | unchanged pin; it now clocks only the pad-edge DDR/gearbox regions. The core runs on an internal word/4 divided clock (×`debug.host_link.rate`) |
| all others | — | unchanged |

## wired_rx capture (`pt_wrx_cap`) — proposed 2026-11-09 (wired_rx owner; needs digital/register confirmation)

| ID | Change | Owners | Interface effect |
|---|---|---|---|
| W1 | **Capture re-capture select, revised 2026-11-09 to 1 bit** `cap_rcs` (`pt_wrx_cap` port `RCS`): 0 = slicer output S_j re-captured on CKP(j+4) (T/2 after sampling, today's behaviour, reset value); 1 = re-captured on CKP(j) one full period after sampling (octet and word one ring cycle later). The earlier 2-bit k ∈ {2,4,6} proposal is withdrawn: k = 2/6 are not uniform in the WCLK_IN fallback (CKP0..3 = CK, CKP4..7 = !CK), where data and edge samplers would need different k, and offsets T/2 and 3T/2 are the same physical capture. Offsets T/2 and T are half a period apart, so one of them clears the slicer delay (0.95–1.63 ns) whenever spread + setup + hold < T/2. Calibrated from raw words (choose the value with 0 PRBS/comma errors and EW consistent with DW). | wired_rx (capture front, implemented in the working copy `scratch/wired_rx/w1`, see wired_rx README), digital (1-bit static field, e.g. `cdr.config.cap_rcs`, reset 0) | +1 static control port `RCS` on `pt_wrx_cap` (integration netlist). With RCS = 1 the LDE → word latency grows by one ring cycle (word boundary moves by bpc bits; harmless for raw words and the CDR). |
| W2 | `pt_wrx_cap` r2 keeps its port list and the LDE → word latency (LDE is now registered twice internally). | wired_rx | None. |

## Digital: W1 allocated; AC-3 CDR controls pending (2026-11-09)

- **W1 done.** Field `cdr.config.cap_rcsel` is 2 bits, stopped-only, reset 1 (k = 4), with codes 0/1/2 giving k = 2/4/6. Its output bits are `analog_cfg[PT_A_CDR_CONFIG__CAP_RCSEL_LSB +: 2]`, and `analog_cfg` grows from 511 to 513 bits, so integration must bind the two bits to `pt_wrx_cap`. The map hash is now 0x097D. `make transceiver-regfile` and `transceiver-regfile-formal` (safe-reset proof) pass, and the chip elaborates.
- **AC-3 controls are not yet allocated:**
  - continuous integral path enable (1 bit);
  - loop bandwidth code 0.5–4 MHz (3 bits);
  - `cdr.phase_trim[7:0][4:0]` (40 bits);
  - histogram readout.
  
  These add about 46 stored bits, or about 92 FF with shadow/active. They go in together with the R3 re-encoding of the 24-bit CDR timeouts, which frees about 64 FF but changes the ABI and the system-model contracts in `test_receive_qualification.py`, `design_definition.py` and `test_design_definition.py`. Current macro headroom is about 85 FF after the 90-FF header reclaim.

## RF TX output (rf_tx owner, 2026-11-09)

- **Board: RF_TX DC feed.** The rfdriver output stage is no longer fed through on-chip resistors. Each RF_TX pad
  must be DC-fed from the rf_tx island rail (3.3 V) through the balun centre tap or two RF chokes, with a DC-blocked
  load behind a 200:50 ohm balun (assumed loss 1.0 dB). The feed must come from the same switched rail as the block
  (otherwise reset/rail-off leaves pads at 3.3 V with the chip unpowered). Without the feed the driver only reaches
  low power (keeper ~1.6 kohm/leg).
- **Current budget: rf_tx driver 28.92 mA -> ~59 mA max** (tt code 15, chip + feed; 41-56 mA over corners;
  ~23 mA at mute). Gain code still scales it (code 4: ~34 mA).
- **Area: rfdriver 111,397 um^2 vs 98,100 ceiling** (+13.3k). Floorplan/integration must grow the slot.
- **Pads (pads/integration owner): rftxpad stress-safe clamp conflicts.** Pads now rest at ~3.3 V with peaks to
  ~4.1 V. The stress-safe clamp's 10 uA onset is 2.1 V (ff) to 3.2 V (ss), so it would conduct continuously and clip.
  rftxpad needs a protection with onset above ~4.4 V (e.g. 06v0-based clamp / steering to a separate higher clamp
  rail). Note the *old* design already sat at 2.3-2.4 V pad CM, above the ff onset.
- **Port list unchanged** (rfdriver/txmix/txsum). `rfdriver.gain[7:4]` slices now weigh 12/24/48/96 tail units.
  Off-state pad pull-down clamps (CLP_*) removed: pads rest at the feed.
- **LO requirements for HE20 at 0 dBm** (quadrature/LO owner): I/Q LO skew <= 5 ps (IRR >= 29 dB) and white LO
  jitter <= 0.5 ps rms to keep the mask.
- **FPGA**: TX needs a digital spectral-shaping FIR (pass 9.7 MHz / stop 10.4 MHz at 40 MS/s); unwindowed OFDM
  fails the 802.11ax mask by ~6 dB regardless of analog.

# Interface changes: RF RX chain owner (analog/rf_rx) — 2026-11-09

## bbfilter_i / bbfilter_q: PGA ports (needs digital/regfile + integration)
- Ports `gain0..gain3` become **`gain0..gain5` + `gainc0`, `gainc1`** (port order in
  `rf_rx/netlists.py:bbfilter`, after `gain3`: `gain4 gain5 gainc0 gainc1`, then `island_off`).
  - `gain[5:0]` = k, the stage-1 input conductance in units of 1/(4R): stage-1 gain = k/4.
  - `gainc0` / `gainc1` = +12 dB each, in biquad 1 / biquad 2.
- `pga_x.gain` (dB, -6..42 in 3 dB steps, 17 codes) maps to the bits through a LUT in the
  digital. The values are in `netlists.GAIN_TABLE`: -6:(2,0,0) -3:(3,0,0) 0:(4,0,0) 3:(6,0,0)
  6:(8,0,0) 9:(12,0,0) 12:(17,0,0) 15:(24,0,0) 18:(35,0,0) 21:(12,1,0) 24:(17,1,0) 27:(24,1,0)
  30:(35,1,0) 33:(12,1,1) 36:(17,1,1) 39:(25,1,1) 42:(36,1,1), each entry (k, gainc0, gainc1).
- **All-zero bits = PGA muted** (no stage-1 input conductance). This is safe, but a 0 dB default
  needs `gain2=1` (k=4). The reset-value choice belongs to the regfile owner.

## bbfilter area and current (integration / power owner)
- Area is 465,219 µm² per filter (I and Q), against a 430,000 allocation; it was 406,239.
  The integration floorplan needs about +35k µm² per filter, or an approved allocation change.
  The limit has not been moved.
- RX-active current is 6.66 mA tt (5.82 before). The added stage-1 OTA is 2x, about +0.45 mA
  per OTA (two per filter). The AA target is 4.5 mA; it already failed before this change.

## ADC input load (to the ADC owner, a64037f)
- The 2-way interleaved ADC (0.44 pF/leg per core, 11 ns tracking, reset to VCM) loads
  `out_rx`.
- Extracted settle at ss/32 °C/3.15 V, 0.78 V differential: the residual is 7.2–8.0 mV,
  which is 1.0–1.2 LSB8, so it **fails** the 1 LSB8 limit.
- A filter-side output buffer (~1–2 mA/leg) is required and is **not built yet (open)**.

## Radio ADC `adc_i` / `adc_q`: v3, 2-way time-interleaved for 40 MS/s (converters owner, 2026-11-10)

Block boundary change. Owners affected: digital (rtl/regmap, RF RX stream), integration (floorplan, routing), clock tree (ADC_CLOCK).
- **New ports** (v2 ports keep their names and meaning for core A):
  `ILEN` (input, after `EN`), `CODEB0..CODEB9`, `DONEB`, `OVERLOADB` (outputs, after `OVERLOAD`).
  Full order: `VDD VSS INP INN VCM VREF IBIAS CLKP CLKN EN ILEN TEST0 TEST1 CODE0..9 DONE OVERLOAD CODEB0..9 DONEB OVERLOADB ATB_BIAS`.
- **New register field** needed: `adc_*.config.interleave` → `ILEN`, reset 0, stopped-only. With `ILEN=0` core B is held in reset and the block behaves like v2 (single core, 20 MS/s, `CODE*`/`DONE`). This is the in-block fallback.
- **ADC_CLOCK:** 40 MHz with 6 ns high time (acquisition) when `ILEN=1`; 20 MHz / 10 ns high for `ILEN=0`. The first SAMPLE pulse after `EN` goes to core A, then the cores alternate.
- **Digital:** each core's code is valid from its own DONE/DONEB rising edge for ≥ 1 clock period (it is held until the same core's next conversion completes, 50 ns later). The RF RX stream must take A and B alternately: 40 MS/s × 10 bit per channel. Per-core offset and gain correction belongs in the FPGA (or the RX DSP). Expect up to ~10 codes of offset difference between the cores, which gives a spur at fs/2 if left uncorrected.
- **Area:** 581k µm² per instance (bbox 468 × 1242 µm), was 259k. The ceiling is 350k, so the block does not fit; integration must find about +645k µm² for I+Q.
- **Supply:** about 2× the v2 current (2 cores); the full number is in the evidence file.
- **Input driver (RX, open):** the RX fork's extracted settle check of the bbfilter output into the alternating 0.44 pF/leg sampling load shows a 1.0–1.2 LSB8 error at 11 ns of tracking (ss). A buffer of about 1–2 mA per leg at the filter output, or an ADC-side input buffer, is needed. **Not built yet.** The ADC currently has 6 ns of acquisition; "track from own DONE" (≥ 11 ns at ss) is a proposed follow-up and is not implemented.

## Radio DAC `dac_i` / `dac_q`: v3 (converters owner)
- No port change.
- **Output CM** is now regulated to VDD/2 by an on-block CM loop (it was VDD − 1.65 V passive: 1.43 V tt, 0.99–1.83 V across corners). The TX filter/mixer input must accept a CM of VDD/2 (1.575–1.675 V).
- **Area:** 192k µm² (was 126k; ceiling 225k).

## RF chain: off-chip passives and pin requests (RF/converters owner, 2026-11-10)
Context: MANDATE.md "Off-chip passives allowed". Board items B17–B21 in spec/board-requirements.md.
- **No new pins** for B17 (TX balun/chokes on the existing RF_TX pair), B18 (RX match on RF_RX), B19 (LO hybrid on REF_IN/AUX_CLK) or B21 (package model).
- **Pin request (optional, pads/assembly owner to decide): 4 pins for an external anti-alias loop** on the primary ADC path. These would carry bbfilter_i/q output differential to the board LC filter and back into adc_i/q INP/INN, with an on-chip bypass switch. Without the pins, the external AA filter exists only on the BB_I/Q tap → external ADC fallback. The 19 spare sites are booked for debug, so this is a request, not an assumption. The on-chip 40 MS/s ADC v3 remains the primary fix for adjacent-channel aliasing.
- **Sim obligation:** RF TX/RX evidence must be rerun with bond wire (1 nH/mm, 1.5 mm nominal), pad C, explicit gate R and the datasheet balun loss. This is open in the RF owner's queue.

## Wired TX `pt_wiredtx` (wired_tx owner, 2026-11-10)

| ID | Change | Owners | Interface effect / request |
|---|---|---|---|
| WT1 | **Rate field code 11 is now defined.** `MODE_H`/`MODE_F`: 00 quarter, 10 half, 01 full run the new compact core (all ring rates); **11 = generic static-CMOS serializer in half rate (fallback route)**. Previously 11 was illegal (F won). | rtl/regfile (`wiredtx` rate field), register-map doc | Field encoding/doc only; no new pins. Reset default stays 00. |
| WT2 | **Equal load on `WTX_PH0/90/180/270`.** The compact core needs ≤ ~10 ps phase-to-phase skew at its inputs (window widths are set from phase spacing). The block now presents identical gate loads on all four phases. | timing (clockmux `WS_*` output, routing to wired_tx) | Route the four phases length-matched (±20 µm) with equal shielding; report the clockmux output skew. |
| WT3 | **Area:** `pt_wiredtx` is now 0.337 mm² (was 0.199; allocation 0.30). The compact core (+~640 devices) is placed by the generic row placer; the generic serializer is kept as the fallback. | integration / floorplan | Allocation +0.04 mm² requested, or the generic fallback serializer removed (−~0.05 mm²) if the fallback policy allows. |
| WT4 | **Swing ≥ 800 mVppd (S8):** the pad output stage is already a current-steering (CML-type) driver; MAIN tail = 83 µA × `wiredtx.config.current_code`. Code 200 → 16.6 mA, 240 → 19.9 mA (DAC full scale 21 mA at 255). | rtl/regfile reset/recipe value; pads (termination on, 50 Ω) | PCIe recipe: `current_code` = 200–240 (default stays 109 until the integration eye at 200/240 is confirmed: `int_serial_q2g5_main200/240`). |
| WT5 | **40-bit boundary (S3) not yet implemented in `pt_wiredtx`.** Plan: one 2:1 word mux in front of the compact core's load muxes (D[i] / D[i+20] alternate per load, toggled in the divider domain), `WORD_CLK` = line/40; +20 input ports `D20..D39`. | wired_tx (next), rtl (S2/S3), integration (ports) | Port list change pending; wired_tx will implement against the S3 port list once the RTL boundary lands. |
| WT6 | **Off-chip passives (board B22) (MANDATE "Off-chip passives allowed"):** TMDS sink pull-ups 50 Ω to 3.3 V are the sink's; for PCIe/SATA/DP an AC-coupling cap per leg (PCIe 75–265 nF, 0402) is assumed in the link budget. No extra pins requested. | board (board-requirements.md) | Listed for the board owner; no pin change. |
| WT7 | Pad capacitance (2.71 pF > 1.6 pF / T-coil), clamp leakage at ff (5.1 mA at 3.3 V on TMDS/FS high levels): **owned by pads** (S7). The wired-TX integration benches use the pads' extracted `tx_protected_pad` and steering buffers read-only, bond 1 Ω / 1 nH per leg. | pads | Re-run `int_*` once pads publish the low-C / T-coil variant. |
| WT8 | **40-bit word boundary implemented (closes WT5; matches S3/T2).** New input ports `D20..D39` and output `WORD_CLK40` (line/40, 50 % duty, from a toggle flop on WORD_CLK; reset with SER_EN low → first WORD_CLK edge sets SEL=1 and WORD_CLK40 rises). D0..D19 are loaded on the first load after WORD_CLK40 rises, D20..D39 on the next; D0 first on the line. `WORD_CLK` (line/20) stays as an output (WCLK_OBS). | digital (`wire_tx_data[39:0]`, `wire_tx_clk` ← WORD_CLK40), integration/assembly (port binding, +20 data nets) | Port list of `pt_wiredtx` grows by 21. |
| WT9 | **Load timing moved from 3 to 4 register periods after `WORD_CLK` rises** (compact core: LD is now registered; it was combinational and missed a 1.235 ns period at 3.24 Gb/s by ~15 ps). One register period = 5 UI at quarter rate, 10 at half, 20 at full. **SDC note for digital:** the 40-bit word is captured by the 20-bit word mux *before* that load; per 20-bit half the capture point is WORD_CLK rising + 4·T_word/5 (was 3·T_word/5) − ~0.4 ns buffer delay, setup ≈ 0.5 ns, hold ≈ 0.3 ns. ## Digital: assembly items (2026-11-10)

**A1. The 23 glue nets are now macro outputs** (rtl/pt_digital_macro.sv):

- `en_eff[19:0]`: `EN_EFF_<blk> = (<blk>.state.state == active(2)) && island_on[k]`. The order, documented in the port comment, is:
  - [0] adc_i, [1] adc_q, [2] wiredtx, [3] dac_i, [4] dac_q
  - [5] refbuf, [6] detect, [7] rfdriver, [8] rxmix_i, [9] rxmix_q
  - [10] pll_cdr, [11] pll_host (rfpll.state), [12] cdr (same decode as [10])
  - [13] slicer, [14] ctle, [15] txmix_i, [16] txmix_q, [17] lna, [18] rxselect, [19] txsum
- `island_off[5:0] = ~island_on`: ISLAND_OFF_wired = [0], _rf = [1], _debug = [5].

Bit positions come from the generated `PT_A_*_STATE__STATE_LSB` macros. The raw `analog_cfg[...]` indices in `binding.json` predate the W1 field, which shifts `analog_cfg` bits above `cdr.config`, so re-derive bindings from `rtl/pt_regmap_defs.svh` or `spec/register-map.json`. The chip elaborates (`make transceiver-regfile`, chip_integration).

**A2. Port list changes since the published outline:**

| Port | Change |
|---|---|
| `debug_cfg` | 243 bits (was 241; adds `debug.host_link.rate`) |
| `analog_cfg` | 513 bits (W1 `cdr.config.cap_rcsel`) |
| `en_eff` | new, 20 bits |
| `island_off` | new, 6 bits |

The S3 change (40-bit wired data) is still to come. The LEF in `scratch/digital-outline/` will be regenerated from the next placed run. Pin edges follow `verification/digital_hardening/pin_order.cfg`: `en_eff`/`island_off` on the east edge with the other configuration outputs. A north-centre macro placement with D2H at the south pads is noted, and the S2 south D2H gearbox region is the agreed fix.

**A3. Register-map assumptions checked:**

- `island_en[3]` (usb) is RESERVED in `spec/debug-controls.json`: constant 0, `island_pgood[3]` tied 0, `island_on[3]=0`. No switch is assumed, so this is consistent with no switch existing.
- No register field assumes a core-bias output for the CDR or a USB bias DAC. The only `core.biasgen` / `bias.*` fields are the biasgen sources/sinks (0x5B–0x5C), `bias.trim` (0x68) and `debug.bias_override`.
- `usbhs.bias` at 0xD7 (`code` 8 bits, `enable`) is a block-local DAC control on the wired island, with no core-bias output assumed (`binding.json` reports `usb_hs_bias_0xD7_bound: true`). If the usbhs HS bias DAC needs a core-bias reference current, that is a core_support output to add. The register field itself is unchanged.

The block owners must confirm that nothing in `pt_rxmix_*`/`pt_txmix_*` depends on orientation, such as a matching-sensitive layout that assumes the same orientation as the I cell. | RF RX, RF TX (confirm) | Reply "mirror OK" or name the orientation-dependent structure. |
| I2 | **LO pin pitch for a symmetric shielded LO route.** Today `txmix_*` `lo_p`/`lo_n` are Metal5 at a 1.04 µm pitch (x 0.52/1.56), which leaves no room for a shield between or beside them. `rxmix_*` has two lo_p/lo_n pairs on Metal3 (8 µm pitch at x 14.6/22.6 y 1.0; 1.2 µm pitch at x 175.4/176.6 y 21.5). The `polyphase` outputs on Metal5 are not in a symmetric order (QP 21.0, IP 55.8, QN 90.6, IN 177.0). Requested pin plan: each LO pair on **one** edge, on Metal4 (signal) with Metal3/Metal5 shields available, **≥ 4 µm pitch** (2 µm wire, 2 µm gap), one GND (VSS) shield pin on each side of the pair. For the polyphase: output order QN QP IP IN along the top edge, mirror-symmetric about the cell centre. | RF RX (`rxmix`), RF TX (`txmix`), timing (`polyphase`) | New pin positions in the next GDS/abstract. Integration then routes the LO as two length-matched, shielded differential pairs, I and Q mirror images. |
| I3 | **Digital macro placement versus D2H.** The macro sits at origin (1080, 3100), north. The D2H gearbox reserve is at the south pad edge (y 452–560). The macro's D2H pins are on its south edge, so the gearbox-to-macro bus would run about 2.5 mm. Integration proposes either (a) flipping the macro pin plan (D2H north, H2D south) and moving the D2H/H2D host-pad assignment to match, or (b) keeping the pin plan and running the word/4 (78 MHz, 40-bit) side of the gearbox over the long distance, with the 312.5 MHz DDR and 156.25 MHz 1:4 confined to the pad-edge region. (b) needs no digital change if the gearbox→core interface is the word/4 bus. | digital | State which side of the S2 gearbox crosses the region boundary (156.25 MHz 1:4 or word/4), and accept (a) or (b). |
| I4 | **Pin budget (task 6):** none of `DBG_OBS1`/`WDBG_OBS`/`WCLK_OBS` is released. No block has requested a crystal or LC-tank pin; the RX/CDR crystal uses `REF_IN` (B23). The RF 4-pin external AA request (B20) is not a crystal/LC-tank pin. | all | Request here if a crystal/LC tank pin is needed. |
| I5 | **WIRE_RX/WIRE_TX P/N bond swap** is documented as board requirement B24. The physical bond map is `assembly.json` `bond_map`. | board, pads | — |

## wired_rx (AC-3 / W1 resume, 2026-11-10)

| ID | Change | Owners | Interface effect |
|---|---|---|---|
| W1-r3 | `pt_wrx_cap` r3 implements the 1-bit `RCS` in the project copy (`analog/wired_rx/capture.py`, data-path mux, all front clocks EN-gated). **Binding onto the already-allocated 2-bit `cdr.config.cap_rcsel` (reset 1):** `RCS = cap_rcsel[1]`. Code 1 (reset) = re-capture T/2 after sampling (r2f behaviour); code 2 = one full period after sampling (the setting the real r2 slicer needs at 810 MHz and 625 MHz ss); codes 0 and 3 alias 1 and 2. The k = 2/6 meanings in the generator should be renamed (no k = 2/6 hardware: not uniform in the WCLK_IN fallback). | digital (rename codes in the field description only; no bit change), integration (bind `cap_rcsel[1]` → `RCS`) | None beyond the earlier +1 port. Octet latency is two ring cycles longer than r2f in both settings; LDE still sets the word boundary. |
| AC3-W1 | **CDR acquisition without HOLD hand-over:** the RX `pll_ring` runs with `HOLD=1` (PFD held in reset) from enable; the RTL FLL drives `bb_up/bb_dn` on the same pump legs until \|err\| ≤ 1000 ppm, then the per-UI PD votes take the same legs. The PFD path of the RX ring is used only in the HDMI/DVI reference mode (below). | digital (FLL keeps driving pump pins; no `freq_hold` sequencing), timing (RX ring HOLD tied by `cdr.config`) | `freq_hold` sequencing in `pt_wrx_cdr_loop.sv` retired for half/quarter rate. |
| AC3-W2 | **Pump gain code** 3 bits: unit leg 8/16/32/64 nA (×4 legs) → estimated BB bandwidth 0.5/1/2/4 MHz at 0.05 UI rms input jitter (`analog/wired_rx/cdr_model.py`). Default 32 nA at 2.5 Gb/s. | digital (`cdr.config.loop_bw[2:0]`, stopped-only) | Replaces `bb_gain`. |
| AC3-W3 | **WCLK_IN → RX FLL reference:** for HDMI/DVI the forwarded TMDS clock is the FLL count reference (not the PFD reference); the BB loop then tracks at 0 ppm like every other protocol. Needs a clockmux select of `WCK` into the RX ring `REF`/REFO counter path (`PS_WCK`, ≤ 150 MHz). | timing clockmux owner, digital (select bit) | +1 one-hot select on the PREF domain. |
| AC3-W4 | **PD latency:** the octet reaches the PD 4 ring cycles after sampling (r3) and the PD adds 2 pipeline stages (6 cycles = 24 UI at quarter rate) vs the AC-3 table's ≤ 2 cycles. Model: dither 32 nA×20 kΩ×Kvco/f = 650 ppm × 24 UI = 0.016 UI; all model cases pass. Proposed requirement: latency ≤ 8 ring cycles. | spec owner | Requirement text only. |

## Digital answers to I6 / I7 / I11 (2026-11-10)

- **I6: adopted** in `verification/digital_hardening/pin_order.cfg`, effective from the next placed run. The routed run CLOSE_R1 still uses the previous plan.
  - S: `rf_*`, `calibration_*`, reset/POR, `host_word_clk`, `d2h_*`, `spi_*`, `wire_*`. The order runs west to east, so `rf_*` lands in the west part and `d2h`/`spi`/`wire` in the east part. The LibreLane order config spreads each edge evenly and cannot pin exact x ranges; if 2243–2975 µm must be exact, integration should give the coordinates and I will switch to a DEF pin template.
  - N: `h2d_*`, `debug_cfg*`.
  - W: `ref_clk`, `ct_*`, `fc_src*`, `cdr_freq_locked`, `clock_trim*`, `enable`, `mode8`, `analog_cfg*`, `island_*`, `en_eff*`, `atb*`, `rx_en_*`, `fault`, `analog_status*`, `commit_toggle`.
  - E: only the four observation enables and the `dbg_obs*`/`wdbg_obs*` pins.
  - I3: option (b) accepted. The word/4 side crosses the corridor.
- **I7: (a) no.** The macro PDN uses Metal4 vertical straps at a 76.8 µm pitch. Every standard-cell row is fed through them, so a Metal4 keep-out would leave rows under it unpowered. Prefer (b). The macro can also add its own decap: about 35–40 % of its 2.43 mm² core is free sites after placement (cell area ≈ 1.58–1.70 mm² in the current runs), which take `fillcap` decap fill cells. The resulting capacitance will be reported from the routed fill once it is extracted. It is not claimed yet.
- **I11: fits.** The S2 D2H side per 11 lanes (10 data + forwarded clock) needs:
  - DDR launch (2 FF/lane)
  - a 4:1 word serializer with a 40-bit holding register and 20-bit staging
  - phase/control (≈5 FF)
  
  That is about 90 FF plus about 2× their area in muxing. At about 75 µm² per FF this is about 20 k µm². The reserve is 2230 × 48 µm = 107 k µm², about 12 rows of 3.92 µm. The binding constraint is clock distribution along the 2.23 mm strip at 156.25 MHz, which CTS treats as a separate local tree. The real size will be published with the S2 RTL.

## Digital: final answers on I7(a) and PLL 3→2 under AC-1 (2026-11-10)

**I7(a) MIM over the macro: not possible without loss or risk.**

- In GF180 the MIM sits between Metal4 (bottom plate) and Metal5 (top plate contact).
- The macro needs both layers. Metal4 carries the PDN vertical straps (76.8 µm pitch, feeding every row) plus signal routing. Metal5 is in use because RT_MAX_LAYER is Metal5: the routed CLOSE_R1 has 4.66 m of signal wire with M5 tracks used.
- A keep-out would either leave rows unpowered (M4) or force re-routing with fewer layers on a macro that does not yet close timing (M5).
- Recommendation: MOS decap in the corridors (I7(b)), plus `fillcap` decap fill inside the macro's 36 % free sites.

**PLL 3→2 (R1): no capability is lost, so R1 can proceed.**

- With AC-1 (host word clock = line/8 from `line_divided`, then the gearbox), every framed wired rate takes its host clock from the TX line ring (Ring L).
- The host link is FPGA-follows-chip: the FPGA derives H2D timing from the forwarded D2H clock. No wired mode therefore needs the chip to lock to the FPGA (`host_follow`), whether the on-chip half/quarter CDR runs on Ring C or not.
- The RX path crosses into the host domain through the `wire_in` async FIFO. Per-frame payload counts with 15.2 % framed headroom at divider 8 absorb RX/TX ppm offset (≤0.6 % with SSC).
- `host_follow` is needed only in RF/passive-LO modes, where Ring C is free.
- The combination "host_follow + on-chip half/quarter CDR" is therefore not a capability any wired mode uses. A wired mode in which the board requires the chip to follow an FPGA clock would be a new requirement, and none exists in `interface-requirements.md` or the S1 rate table.

## Risk-register items accepted by RF/converters owner (2026-11-10)
- **SUB-01 (adc_i isolation), converters v4, planned:** DNW pockets under the adc_i NMOS within 300 µm of the digital macro. Body on the isolated p-well; DNW/p-well and DNW/p-sub junctions are added by hand as PDK diodes for simulation, and LVS handling is to be checked. A ≥5 µm p+ ring on the north edge on the ADC's own VSS. The sampling edge is phased away from the macro clock edge (needs the clock-tree owner). Area is about +15–25k µm² on top of 581k. **Assembly:** the north-edge strap and the v4 floorplan distance. **Digital/clock:** the ADC_CLOCK sampling-edge phase relative to the macro clock. The ss/ff ADC FFTs are deferred until v4 so they run on the final layout.
- **XT-02 (LNA→mixer I/Q feeds), rf_rx + assembly:** the two feeds must present equal C (route length/width matched, or a dummy C on the shorter feed). rf_rx will publish the allowed feed-C mismatch once the current rf_rx rerun ends (rf_rx sources are frozen while it runs).
- **A7 / TX mask at 30 MHz (~0 dB margin):** the RF/converters owner takes the DAC side. The DAC image at fs−f is attenuated only by sinc (ZOH) and the BB filter. Options are an 80 MS/s update (SP-03, the DAC passes 80 MS/s static codes) or a lower TX filter code. Waiting for the A7 result.
- **Compacted pads (+40–113 fF on RF pad nodes):** the RF_RX/RF_TX match screens will be rerun with the new pad C plus a 1.5 mm bond wire (B21) once the pads owner publishes the pad netlist.

## Digital answers to risk-register items (2026-11-10)

**(1) Macro decap: about 3 nF is not reachable with library cells. Quantified as follows:**

- The routed CLOSE_R1 already fills every free site with library decap:
  - `fillcap_4/8/16/32/64`: 14934/7523/3349/806/2347 cells, which is 87,376 nfet+pfet gate pairs (PDK SPICE: W 0.82+1.22 µm, L 1 µm).
  - These are 5 V-oxide devices (`nfet_05v0`, about 2.3 fF/µm²), so about 4.7 fF per pair, or **about 0.4 nF total**.
- Library fillcap density is about 0.5 fF/µm² of site area, so 3 nF would need about 5.6 mm².

**Proposal (no capability loss):** a custom standard-cell-compatible decap cell on thin-oxide `nfet_03v3`/`pfet_03v3`. The core rail is 3.15–3.35 V, inside 3v3 device ratings. At about 4.9 fF/µm² with about 70 % gate fill, that is about 3.4 fF/µm², and the macro's about 0.87 mm² of fill area would give about 2.9 nF.

This needs a new cell (layout, LEF, GDS, LVS, leakage/reliability check) owned by core_support or the cell owner. Once its LEF/GDS exist, the digital flow can use it as `DECAP_CELL`. Until then the macro provides about 0.4 nF.

**(2) Done.** `hostio.controls.slew` now resets to 1 (slow). The drive reset stays at code 1, the lower of the wired settings. RF modes therefore run slow-slew by default, and the wired full-rate recipe sets `slew=0` and raises drive explicitly. D2H outputs are disabled at reset anyway (`d2h_enable=0`). The map hash is 0x179E; `make transceiver-regfile` and `transceiver-regfile-formal` pass.

**(3) Done.** In RTL, `rf_tx_sample` is forced to 0 whenever `rf_tx_valid=0` (rtl/pt_digital.sv), so the DAC data bus is static throughout receive-only operation, underrun and mute.

**(4) Not controllable from the macro.** The ADC sample clock (`rf_rx_clk`) is generated by the analog clocktree (`clocktree.config.sample_divider`). The digital macro only receives it, so its edge relative to the macro's core clock edges is set in the clocktree. A phase-select field could be added if the clock owner provides a selectable phase tap. The register map can then carry it.

**Pin plan flexibility for v4:** the pin plan is one file (`verification/digital_hardening/pin_order.cfg`) and does not change any RTL, so an edge or corner placement only needs a new side assignment and a re-run.

# Block KLayout DRC / antenna cleanup (I10 follow-up), 2026-11-10

Tooling (all blocks): `make transceiver-block-kldrc BLOCK=<name>|all [PUBLISH=1]` (or `GDS=… TOP=… NAME=…`) runs the tapeout precheck's own decks on one block GDS (gf180mcu.drc, variant D, deep, KLayout 0.30.9 from the pinned analog image; main = `all,-antenna,-density,-cup`, antenna = `antenna`). With PUBLISH=1 it writes `<gds dir>/<block>-kldrc.json` (GDS sha256, per-rule counts). `make transceiver-block-kldrc-check` fails unless every registered block (`verification/kl_block_drc_blocks.json`) has a clean record that matches its current GDS. `make transceiver-gds-collisions` fails on one cell name with different geometry in two block GDS inputs. Each flow's publish step now calls `analog/gds_uniquify.publish_checked()` (prefix all subcells with the block top name, then the KLayout precheck record). Timing evidence requires that record for `clean`.

**Finding:** run on each block GDS by itself, every block's main deck is 0 except the pad families and `attached_tx_buffer`. The I10 r1 counts for the island switches, adc_i and the bias cells came from the r1 PDN overlapping the blocks' own cuts. They are gone in the v3 chip run as well (70,161 items in v3: about 68k in the pad families and the ring, 9 in `pt_chip_top`, 3 in `pt_pdn`).

| # | Change | Owner(s) | Ask |
|---|---|---|---|
| K1 | **Antenna diodes in the generators.** New shared `analog/antenna_diodes.py`. It traces each gate net layer by layer, as the deck does: the partial net of metal ≤ N only. Where perimeter × thickness / gate area exceeds 200 (half the 400 limit), it places an N+/Psub `diode_nd2ps_03v3` (0.36–3.9 µm², about 0.4–4 fF, reverse biased) under the net's own M3/M4/M5 wire. Each diode has its own P+ substrate tap (DF.14_LV), and the LVS reference gets matching `D… diode_nd2ps_03v3 area= pj=` lines. Sim headers now load `diode_<corner>` (timing_flow, wrx_flow, rfrx_flow, rf_tx/simulate, wired_tx/tools). Hooked into `timing/layout_engine.py` (pll_ring, slicer top, clk_obs, ring_osc and all timing blocks), `wired_rx/wrx_flow.py` (cdr, ctle, …), `rf_rx/rfrx_flow.py` (per subcircuit: `port_info` carries protection up the hierarchy), `rf_tx/layoutlib.py`, `converters/lib/convkit.py` (macro inputs use LEF ANTENNAGATEAREA), `wired_tx/layoutgen.py` and `pads/layout.py`. `pads/extract_audit.py` now exports `ndiode` devices. | wired RX (pll_ring, slicer, cdr, ctle), RF (bbfilter_i/q, rfdriver), converters (adc_i/q, dac_i/q), pads | Regenerate and rerun LVS and sims. # Risk-register analyses A1–A8: resulting demands (risk owner, 2026-11-10)

Each row cites the register ID and the analysis. Scripts and raw results are in `physical-limits/analyses/`. The register is regenerated by `physical-limits/gen_register.py`.

| # | Register ID / analysis | Owner | Demand |
|---|---|---|---|
| RA-1 | SUB-04 / A6 | digital, assembly, spec | `aosc.EN` is **unbound** in v3 (floating gate) and has no register field. Its only consumers are fc_src[4] and debug obs. Digital: add `core.aosc.enable` (live, reset 1) and force it to 0 while RF payload runs. Assembly: bind EN to it. Spec: block-diagram ALWAYS_ON_CLOCK → management_clock edges do not exist in RTL; correct them. |
| RA-2 | LU-02 / A6 | assembly | v3 has **no iso_clamp instances**. These are gate nets driven from a switched island into another domain: LO_IP/IN/QP/QN (pll→rf mixers), WMON_IN (wired→debug), fc_src[0..3], analog_status[0], ct_wclk, ct_word_clk (→macro). Place iso_clamps following island_on (list: `analyses/a6_lu02_nets.json` `iso_needed`). |
| RA-3 | LU-02 / A6 | digital | Interlocks: rx_wtx_ext enable AND island_on[wired] (WTX_EXT_DATA lands on wiredtx p-diff); reject ATB codes whose island is not pgood. |
| RA-4 | LU-01 / A6 | pads | `stress_safe_rail_shunt_v2` (in all 5 families): 7,193 µm² of 6 V n-diff more than 15 µm from a p+ tap (3,664 µm² more than 20 µm). Add tap stripes through the finger array, and close the n+ ring around the remaining 4–32 % of p-diff. Criterion: `analyses/lu01/lu.py` shows 0 µm² uncovered at 15 µm. |
| RA-5 | CDC-01 / A6 | digital (STA) | Declare rx/tx_boundary.half as generated clocks of host_rx/tx_clk, and egress_clk as a logically exclusive mux of rf_tx_clk/wire_tx_clk. |
| RA-6 | AN-01, XT-05 / A6 | assembly | Before v4 route sign-off, run the antenna deck on the top level, and keep a 20 µm digital/clock keep-out along the adc_i/adc_q east edges (analog nets only with a grounded shield). |

## Package channel (TT01 COB): placement-tolerance demands (package analysis, 2026-11-10)

Basis: `spec/package-channel.md` (+ `.json`), risks PKG-01..08, board BP-1..BP-8. The die sits on the fixed wafer.space TT01 70-pin mezzanine COB.
- Bonds are 2.2–3.2 mm.
- Traces are 87 Ω, 3–7.5 mm, with one via.
- The 0.4 mm connector adds ≈ 1 nH.
- The COB has no signal passive footprints, so every signal passive sits on the carrier, 2–50 mm past the connector.

Policy: the package shifts performance, and the chip must **tolerate where users put passives**. The qualification axis is "passive placement tolerance": P1–P4 = 2/10/30/50 mm, bond ±0.3 mm, pad C ±20 %.
No pins are added. Owners: please confirm, or answer with a quantified counter-proposal.

**All owners**
- D-ALL-1: replace the 1.5 nH bond model with the per-pin chain in `package-channel.json` (BP-1). Re-run every bench that claims RF/LO/wired/PDN numbers at P1 and P3.

**timing (lo_ext / polyphase): external LO**
- D-LO-1 (PKG-05): an amplitude-tolerant LO input.
  - Programmable per-leg shunt termination: off / 200 / 100 / 50 Ω. 50 Ω cuts the pad-C sensitivity from 8.0 to 2.1 dB.
  - On-level must stay in window across ≥ 10 dB of source level, by limiting or a level-adaptive VB.
  - An LO amplitude detector readable on ATB/ADC for B31 setting.
- D-LO-2 (PKG-04): LO I/Q phase trim of at least ±15° in ≤ 1° steps, plus I/Q amplitude trim ±1 dB.
  - The COB alone gives −6 to −10° at the pads, independent of carrier distance.
  - Calibrate it with the TX→RX loopback image measurement.
- D-LO-3: re-run `lo_ext_pad.py` with the COB chain (REF bonds 2.44/2.42 mm, AUX 2.47/2.53 mm, traces per JSON) and republish the B3 window.
  - My estimate: +2.5 dB, ≈ 4.0–5.3 Vpp open-circuit, if the pad load is ≈ 4 pF.
- D-LO-4 (fallback for users without a hybrid): single-ended / one-source LO mode. The N leg is AC-grounded on chip (≥ 3 pF MIM), and Q comes from the existing polyphase.
  - On-chip AC coupling (≥ 2 pF MIM per leg, bypassable) so a DC-coupled source is safe.

**rf_rx**
- D-RX-1: a real input of ≈ 100–200 Ω per leg with a programmable shunt C trim (0–0.5 pF in ≥ 8 steps). The match then re-centres for any placement.
  - Analysis: RL ≥ 26.9 dB in band at every placement with a 0.5 pF ∥ 200 Ω input. Loss is 0.37/0.61/1.44/2.2 dB at P1–P4.
  - The old ≈ 4 Ω high-Q LNTA input would not tolerate this.
- D-RX-2 (fallback, no board balun): single-ended RX mode, with RF_RX_N AC-grounded on chip by a switch plus ≥ 5 pF MIM. On-chip 5 pF DC block, bypassable.

**rf_tx**
- D-TX-1: output stage tolerant of a load at P1–P4 (Pout −0.26…−2.3 dB from placement). Programmable output resistance or back-off code range covering ±3 dB.
- D-TX-2 (fallback, no board balun): single-ended output mode, with one leg into an on-chip dummy load (−3 dB Pout).
- D-TX-3 (PKG-03): see assembly D-AS-1. The bond crosstalk RF_TX_P→AUX_CLK_N is estimated at −17.5 dB SE at 2.45 GHz.

**wired_rx / pads**
- D-WR-1 (PKG-02): on-die termination trim from 40 to 60 Ω per leg in ≤ 2.5 Ω steps.
  - RL at 1.25 GHz is 10.0 dB today (2.35 pF) and 14.8 dB at 1.6 pF/55 Ω. PCIe RX needs ≥ 10 dB, so there is 0 dB margin today.
  - Keep the S7 pad-C target ≤ 1.6 pF, and reach ≤ 1.2 pF if a T-coil fits (I8).
- D-WR-2: CTLE peaking range 0–12 dB at 1.25 GHz in ≤ 1 dB steps.
  - The package plus P4 carrier uses 1.2 dB + 1.2 dB + pad roll-off ≈ 3 dB.
  - The remainder covers the user's cable and reference channel.
- D-WR-3 (fallback, users who DC-couple): RX input common-mode range of at least 0.4–2.4 V with termination on, so AC caps are optional for TMDS-like and short links.
  - On-chip AC coupling is not feasible for 8b/10b: the HPF corner of a 20 pF cap is 160 MHz.

**wired_tx**
- D-WT-1: TX de-emphasis 0 to −6 dB in ≥ 4 steps (1 post-tap).
- D-WT-2: swing headroom. The carrier sees ≈ 1–1.5 dB less than the die at 1.25 GHz, so 800 mVppd at the connector needs ≈ 900–950 mVppd at the die.
  - Extend the `current_code` range to ≥ 24 mA, or record 700 mVppd as the guaranteed carrier swing.
- D-WT-3: termination trim 40–60 Ω (as D-WR-1).

**assembly (pin map, v4)**
- D-AS-1 (PKG-03): separate RF_TX and AUX_CLK by ≥ 1 site on the west edge, by swapping BB_Q and RF_TX (BB is a low-rate debug tap).
  - New west order (inputs[11]→[0]): RF_RX, BB_I, REF, AUX, BB_Q, RF_TX.
  - RF_TX then lands on PCB pads 55/56, connector 26/27, next to GND contact 25.
  - Also keep REF_IN off BB_I_N if the BB tap is active in RF mode, or disable the BB tap during RF reception.
- D-AS-2: fastest signals to the shortest COB traces with adjacent GND contacts.
  - Already true for D2H_CLK/H2D_CLK (2.9 mm, contacts 1/36 next to tabs and GND 10).
  - For a v5 map, the shortest COB paths are bond pads 7–10, 21–24, 27–32 (odd), 44–47, 58–60. Use the JSON `cob_delay_ps` column.
  - No v4 move is demanded beyond D-AS-1.
- D-AS-3 (PKG-06): on-die decap ≥ 3–4 nF total (PDN-02 fill) and a damped bank (series R ≈ 0.5–1 Ω aggregate) so that Z_die ≤ 2 Ω over 30–400 MHz.
  - The package resonance is 107–159 MHz (Q 12–28) with 1 nF, which is on top of the 156.25 MHz host clock.

**digital (host link)**
- D-DG-1 (PKG-08): D2H defaults are slow slew (≥ 1.2 ns 20–80) and drive ≤ 30 mA, in all modes, not only RF. Full drive gives ≈ 490 mV bounce against the 300 mV limit.
- D-DG-2: optional 2-group D2H launch stagger (~200 ps) as a register bit, off by default.
- D-DG-3: no change for the COB 22–27 ps group spread, because FPGA per-bit deskew (HL-B5) covers it.

**core_support / calibration**
- D-CAL-1: a calibration sequence that sets, per board, LO termination and level, I/Q phase/amplitude trim, RX shunt trim, TX back-off and wired termination/CTLE/de-emphasis from on-chip measurements (loopback, detectors, ATB).
  - Store the results in the register file. This is what makes the placement tolerance claims testable.
| PKG-W1 | **Package-channel demands accepted by wired_rx (spec/package-channel.md), not yet implemented:** (a) on-die termination trim 40–60 Ω. Today the pad bank has fixed resistors and `pt_termination` has no trim; this needs ≥ 3-bit switched legs in the pad bank (pads owner) plus the `termination.config.trim[2:0]` register (digital). (b) CTLE range 0–12 dB. Today the extracted peaking is 1.6–8.1 dB across corners (7.3 dB nominal, code 3); this needs a second stage or a stronger degeneration code (wired_rx). (c) Wide input CM for DC-coupled users. Today the slicer is qualified at CM 1.0 V and 0.2 V (USB); the CTLE CM range has not been characterized. (d) Qualify at P1–P4 (carrier 2/10/30/50 mm, bond ±0.3 mm) with the scratch/cob models. The wired_rx benches have no package model yet. RL with 2.35 pF is 9.0–10.3 dB over R 40–60 Ω, so the pad-C reduction (S7) remains the real fix (pads). | wired_rx (CTLE, benches), pads (R trim legs, C), digital (trim field) | +3 static bits |

## RF/converters owner, round 4 (2026-11-10, ~06:50 UTC): Q-trim, ADC buffer, LNA, package demands

All numbers below are scratch candidates (`scratch/rfconv-owner/`, log in `NOTES.md`). **Nothing is published yet:** rf_rx sources and `generated/` are unchanged, so the published evidence stays consistent. Promotion = one `run_rf_rx.sh` rerun once the LNA choice below closes.

They switch 4·Rq and 2·Rq damping resistors (TG on the virtual-ground side) across Rq of the Q = 1 biquad only. Extracted (scratch layout, DRC 0, LVS unique), PGA 0 dB widest, peaking / f3dB: ff/32/3.15 qt0 6.39 dB/31 MHz → **qt2 0.41 dB/29 MHz, qt3 0.00/27 MHz**; tt qt0 1.16/21.8 → **qt1 0.00/20.1**; ss/32/3.15 qt0 0.16/16.1, **qt1 0.00/14.3**. Published (no trim): ff 4.0–5.2 dB, which fails ≤ 1 dB. The diagnosis: shrinking the fixed excess-phase lead MIM makes ff worse (5.2 → 7.5 → 9.9 dB), so ff is under-compensated, and no single fixed lead fits all corners. ## Digital answer to I12 (re-aspect) and macro decap (2026-11-10)

**I12: feasible. 1716 × 1455 µm is adopted for the next placed run.**

- Area is unchanged (2.497 mm²).
- Utilization is about 64 % after routing (CLOSE_R1/R2 at 1580²: route DRC 0, no congestion overflow), so a 1.18:1 aspect is routable.
- Timing is placement-limited by long FIFO/frame paths, not by aspect. The change is neutral to slightly positive, because pins shift to the long N/S edges where the host pads are.
- The H2D DDR capture flops are placed in the top rows: the `h2d_*` pins are on N (pin_order.cfg), and the S2 pad-edge region becomes a top-row placement region when the AC-1 RTL lands. No separate 24 µm strip is needed.
- ≤1393 µm tall (for example 1792 × 1393) is also acceptable for digital. That is 1.29:1 with the same area and utilization. The digital flow has no objection if integration prefers the 300 µm ADC target.

**Macro decap: the routed fill is about 0.4 nF.** The library only has 5 V-oxide `fillcap` cells, and every free site is already filled (14934/7523/3349/806/2347 of `fillcap_4/8/16/32/64`).

2.5–2.9 nF needs a thin-oxide (`nfet_03v3`/`pfet_03v3`) decap cell. None exists in `gf180mcu_fd_sc_mcu7t5v0`. Once a cell owner provides LEF, GDS and LVS netlist (estimated about 3.4 fF/µm² × 0.87 mm² of free sites ≈ 2.9 nF), the digital flow adopts it as `DECAP_CELL`/fill with no RTL change. Until then the effective on-macro decap is 0.4 nF, plus the cells' intrinsic capacitance, which is not counted.
| RC-8a | **Update to RC-8 (08:21): the split I/Q LNA is the pick.** Stage 2 is two transconductors (4 mA each), one per mixer, so the I and Q quads no longer share an RF node. With 50 %-duty LOs that shared node put each TIA's virtual ground behind the other's, which was the dominant noise. Chain NF (same bench as RC-8): **4.45 dB (5–9 MHz) / 5.97 dB (2–9 MHz)**, gain 41.7 dB, LNA 11.7 mA. The first-cut C-trim/SE network (code 0) is included. Bigger shared stage 2 (12 mA) got worse: 7.89 dB. Adding the package doc's placement loss: 4.8 / 5.1 / 5.9 / 6.7 dB at P1–P4 (5–9 MHz). **Port change (rf_rx + integration):** `pt_lna` gets `rf_outq_p/n` after `rf_out_n`. `rf_out_*` → rxmix_i `rf_*`, `rf_outq_*` → rxmix_q `rf_*` (XT-02 feed matching now applies per feed). Also `ct0..ct2`, `se` after `byp`. | rf_rx (owner); integration; digital (`lna.ctrim[2:0]` reset 4, `lna.se` reset 0) | Integration: two LNA→mixer feeds instead of one shared node. |

## Digital status and queue (2026-11-10, after CLOSE_R2)

- **Routed signoff (CLOSE_R2, current sources):**
  - DRC, LVS and antenna 0; GLS pass; 8150 FF.
  - Setup does not close: in-window ss −16.4 ns, tt −7.7 ns. Hold is met.
  - LEF: `scratch/digital-outline/pt_digital_macro.lef`.
  - `config.json` now uses DIE_AREA 1716 × 1455 (I12) for the next run.
- **PD8 (double drivers):** in RTL (`integration/pt_chip.sv`), `ct_word_clk` and `island_on` have exactly one driver each: `pt_digital_macro` outputs into `pt_analog` inputs. The duplicate is in the v3 assembly binding. That binding must not also attach the clocktree/island-switch outputs to these nets.
- **PD9 and the other open requests are queued; not implemented in this pass:**
  - ring-regulator bypass bits (reset bypassed) with a ≥50 µs post-pgood PLL-enable gate (needs a ref_clk timer, about 14 FF);
  - SUB-04 aosc enable, forced off in RF payload;
  - LU-02 interlocks (WTX_EXT_DATA, and ATB codes into unpowered islands) as generator rules;
  - RA-5: the derived clocks already declared are hpair, hcore and egress mux. The freq-counter source mux and clocktree outputs are still to declare;
  - SP-01 per-channel macro clock (`debug.host_link.rate` and the clocktree divider already cover part of it);
  - SP-03 2× halfband interpolator (about 150 FF; needs the R3 reclaim);
  - optional D2H launch stagger;
  - register-driven per-board calibration sequencer;
  - RF fields qtrim, LNA ct/se and TX single-ended.
  
  The macro has 42 FF of headroom (8150/8192), so R3 comes first.

| T2 | **Wired boundary 40 bit (S3), confirms AC3-W5/AC3-W10 and WT5.** `wire_rx_data[39:0]` ← `pt_wrx_cap.DX[39:0]`, `wire_rx_clk` ← `WCLK40` (sampled on its rise; constrained as a primary 62.5 MHz clock with 20 % input delay). `wire_tx_data[39:0]` → `pt_wiredtx.D0..D39`, `wire_tx_clk` ← `pt_wiredtx.WORD_CLK40`. D0..D19 are launched from WORD_CLK40-**falling** flops and D20..D39 from rising flops, so D0..D19 have T40/2 + 0.4·T20 before their load and D20..D39 hold through the second load. valid/request/idle are per 40-bit quad. | digital, wired_rx, wired_tx, integration | Integration: `pt_chip.sv` nets widened (done in RTL, **shared, minimal**); assembly binding of DX0..39 / D0..39 / WCLK40 / WORD_CLK40. |
| T3 | **PRBS/pattern at W=40.** The boundary is 40 bit, so `pt_digital.sv` instantiates `pt_pattern_gen`/`pt_pattern_check` with `W=40` on the 62.5 MHz quad clocks (**shared, minimal**: only the width parameter and the `cfg_user` replication change; `pt_pattern.sv` is untouched). Mode 5 (slow) becomes 40 ones / 40 zeros. | other digital agent (PRBS checker timing at W=40, docs) | Please own the W=40 checker timing (popcount over 40 bits) and the register-map text for mode 5/user word. |

AC-1 target: ≤ 3,750 FF for the same function (FIFO bits unchanged: wire_in 32×40, egress 16×48, rf_in 4×48, IQ TX 6×40, IQ RX 4×40, H2D 4×40). That leaves the other agent ≈ 4,440 FF of the 8,192 cap (today ≈ 4,480 incl. regfile 1,660 + config outputs ~350, memory 1,585, freq counters 545, PRBS ~160, clocktree 72). | both digital agents | Please reply with your reclaim (R3) and queued-feature totals so we can fix the split. |
| T5 | **Library.** RTL stays library-agnostic: every kept physical cell (clock muxes/buffers) is behind one `ifdef SYNTHESIS` wrapper. Pipelining targets ≥ 10 % setup margin at the 1.54× in-window ss derate on gf180mcu_fd_sc_mcu7t5v0 (user decision 2026-11-10). If your in-window Liberty lowers the derate, tell me the number and I will not add further stages. | both digital agents | Send the characterized ss_window factor when available. |


## Digital transport owner: AC-1 resume, pass 2 (2026-11-10)

Supersedes T1 and T4/T5 above where they differ. Owner: transport-core agent. Shared files touched minimally are marked.

| # | Change | Owner(s) | Ask |
|---|---|---|---|
| T6 | **No macro logic at 312.5 MHz; the host clock input is the host DDR clock.** The characterized in-window ss library (D14) gives dffrnq_1 clk→Q 1.86 ns + setup 0.84 ns: a reset toggle at 3.2 ns has ~0 % margin and any 156.25 MHz half-period path ≤ 10 %. `host_word_clk` (macro port name kept) now carries **line/16 = 156.25 MHz at 2.5 Gb/s** (one ten-bit word per pin per clock *edge*); `pt_host_rate_clock` (/1 /2 /4) runs at that rate and yields `hddr`; `hcore` = hddr/2 (78.125 MHz). Falling-edge flops only ever take registers held two periods (1.5-period multicycles, proven: `verification/formal/pt_d2h_gearbox_fv.sv`, `pt_h2d_gearbox_fv.sv`); the only half-period paths are one direct flop→flop phase copy per group and reset release into falling-edge flops (design proven insensitive to which edge). | timing/clocking (clocktree host divider, `debug.clk_select` host source), integration, FPGA | **Clocking:** deliver line/16 (and line/32, line/64 equivalents are made inside the macro by `rate`) on the host-clock net instead of line/8; IX-2(a) "fixed 312.5 MHz host clock" becomes a **fixed 156.25 MHz host DDR clock** option (e.g. host ring 625 MHz ÷4, or REF-derived), same 312.5 Mb/s per pin. **Register map (other digital agent):** `debug.host_link.rate` text: divides the host DDR clock. |
| T7 | **D2H forwarded clock is edge-aligned DDR** (like a DDR DQS): `D2H_CLK` = c_r ^ c_f built exactly like a data pin (flop toggles + XOR), so data/clock skew is structural. The FPGA centres it with its input delay / phase training (already required by spec/fpga-host-screen.md "phase training"). SDR forwarded clocks (`debug.host_link.mode` 1/2) exist only at `rate` div2/div4 (the full-rate SDR word clock would be 312.5 MHz, above the pad f_max). | FPGA, spec (fpga-host-screen.md, register-map text for mode) | FPGA: DDR capture with a quarter-period (UI/2) delay on D2H_CLK. GLS loopback bench now models that delay (`sim/tb_digital_gl.sv`, **shared, minimal**) and runs SDR modes at div2 (`gls_commands.py`, **shared, minimal**). |
| T8 | **IX-2(b) 8b/10b offload implemented** (`rtl/pt_linecode.sv`, tables `rtl/pt_linecode_tables.svh`, reference `verification/linecode_8b10b.py`, check `verification/check_linecode.py` incl. a 3.125 Gb/s end-to-end loopback at 97.6 % host load). RX: K28.5 full-group alignment over 10 bit offsets (new offset adopted on two agreeing commas, so K28.7 false commas cannot misalign), 10b→9b {K, byte}, invalid → {K=1, 0x00} + sticky code error, 36→40 repacking; TX: 40→36 unpacking, RD carried as a parallel prefix of per-symbol disparity flags. Running-disparity errors are not checked; idle (‖I‖/ALIGN) deletion is **not** implemented (3.125 Gb/s fits at 97.6 % without it). Select: stopped-only level, macro input **`wire_linecode`** (strapped 0 in `integration/pt_chip.sv`, **shared, minimal**). | other digital agent (register generator), integration | Add field `wire.config.linecode` (raw/8b10b, stopped-only) and bind it to `wire_linecode` (or move the input into pt_digital). Status bits 4/5 are owner-multiplexed: wired {5 aligned, 4 code error (sticky)}, RF {5 gain pending, 4 gain rejected (sticky)}: please name them in `status`. |
| T9 | **RXR-D2 fast gain (opcode4) implemented.** H2D: legal only under RF ownership with wc=qc=0, arg[7]=0, PGA ≤ 16; one pending command, a command while pending is rejected (sticky status, gain unchanged), never deferred. The gain is applied in the RF RX sample domain (`pt_gain_rx`), which at the same clock discards the packer reservoir and the next 20 samples (0.5 µs settling), restarts packing at a quad boundary and marks the ingress write pointer; the D2H engine finishes the older samples and sends the opcode4 gain-status frame (argument = gain | gap bit6, wc = gap[5:0], qc = new-gain words) exactly as `verification/rf_gain_command.py` GainSampleReceiver expects. Measured (`verification/check_fast_gain.py`): frame start on the H2D pins → gain applied 189 ns (full rate) / 311–331 ns (div2); FPGA decision → gain ≤ 0.4 / 0.74 µs including frame alignment (≤ 1 µs, RF-RX-AGC). Applied to hardware by `pt_digital_macro` while active (cleared by ENABLE=0): LNA index 0 → `lna.gain` −6 dB, 1..3 → 0 dB (10/20 dB not built); PGA index k (−6+3k dB) → bbfilter gain code nearest 20·log10(1+c), 0..15. | rf_rx (RXR-D1 table), other digital agent (register readback), FPGA | RF owner: when RXR-D1 adds tia_gain/lna.att, extend the macro mapping table, not the command. Blocker-detector status bits: no spare bits in the gain-status header (argument bit7 carries gap bit6); proposal: a D2H opcode5 "RF status" frame (metadata-only, wc/qc carry 12 status bits) once the RF owner defines them. |
| T10 | **Wired boundary unchanged** from T2 (D0..D39 / DX0..DX39, WORD_CLK40, WCLK40); STA period now **12.3 ns** (3.24 Gb/s, IX-1 continuous range top) for `wire_tx_clk`/`wire_rx_clk`. | other digital agent | The PRBS checker (popcount/mismatch) and generator must meet 12.3 ns (screen: checker ≈ −7 ns at ss32 zero-wire). |
| T11 | **FF budget (screen synth, yosys counts):** transport 3,984 (ingress 28×40, egress 24×40, H2D 8×40, 8b/10b 381, opcode4 ≈ 70, framers/gearboxes), other side 4,439 → **8,423 > 8,192**. Within 3,760 the 8b/10b offload plus the ss32 pipelining do not fit: ingress 24/26 overflowed at the 3.125 Gb/s start-up transient, egress 20 faulted the slow-wire bench. | both digital agents, user | Evaluated, not done: moving the 2×(32×24) capture/playback memories to `gf180mcu_fd_ip_sram` would free 1,536 FF but costs area: the smallest macro is 432×233 µm (0.10 mm², 64×8) — 3 per memory (8-bit wide) = 0.60 mm² for both vs ≈ 0.15 mm² of flops+muxes today, and a 233 µm-tall hard block in a 1,455 µm-tall macro. Cheaper option for the other agent: 24-entry memories (−384 FF) or 16 entries (D12, −768). |

# Added interfaces (2026-11-10): new hardware demands (spec owner)

User-approved additions: SATA Gen2, DP HBR, 2500BASE-X / XAUI lane, BLE LE Coded, BLE Channel Sounding,
Thread/Zigbee/Matter profiles, continuous wired line-rate coverage. Requirements: `spec/interface-requirements.md`
§1.9–1.13, §2.1, §3.3 (WR-HL-LINECODE); traced in `spec/performance-requirements.json` (35 new rows).
Reused PHY rows are listed in the reuse maps there; only the demands below are new. Envelope: tt/ss/ff × 22–32 °C × 3.15–3.35 V.

| # | Change | Owner(s) | Concrete ask | Requirement IDs |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| IX-1 | **Ring/CDR/serializer range extension for gap-free 0.48–3.24 Gb/s.** Today's ring modes are 480/625/810 MHz (gaps 0.81–0.96 and 1.62–1.92 Gb/s; DP RBR on the 810 edge; 3.125 G at 781 MHz, 3.6 % from the edge). Gap-free with 3 % edge margin needs f_max ≥ 2·f_min·1.03²: **ring 393–834 MHz on the regulated rail** at ss/32 °C/3.15 V (coarse band codes allowed). The top needs the ring resize already named in `analog/pll_vreg/README.md` (≥ 900 MHz at 2.6 V ss; today the regulated ss top is 733–778 MHz and 810 MHz runs only in BYP with ≈ 29–33 ps pk supply DJ). The bottom needs VCTRL ≈ 1.0–1.1 V at ff or one extra coarse band (`vco_replica` reaches 258 MHz at 0.9 V tt; ss/tt do not oscillate at 0.8 V). | timing (pll_ring, pll_vreg), wired_rx (samplers, wrx_cap, cdr), wired_tx (4:1 mux, CML 2:1), timing (wordclk), digital (clocktree band/mode select, FLL search over bands) | Continuous VCTRL + coarse-band lock over 393–834 MHz with ≥ 3 % overlap between bands; S4 samplers and `wrx_cap` resolve at 834 MHz (was 810); 4:1 mux + CML stage to 3.34 Gb/s (fails 1.92–2.97 today); `pt_wordclk` CK→LD < 1.20 ns at 834 MHz (≈ 1.15 ns tt today); FLL lock range covers every band; register field for band code (stopped-only). | WR-RATE-CONT, WR-RATE-EDGE, WR-SATA2-*, WR-DPHBR-*, WR-3G1-* |
| IX-2 | **Host-link fit above 2.5 Gb/s.** Dense capacity is 2.88 Gb/s per direction (10 × 312.5 Mb/s × 59/64). S1 (host word = line/8) needs 337.5/375/390.6 MHz at 2.7/3.0/3.125 Gb/s, above the 312.5 MHz budget. Raw bits: 2.7 G = 94 %, 3.0 G = 104 %, 3.125 G = 109 % of capacity. **3.125 Gb/s does not fit through the AC-1 host link as raw line bits.** | digital (wired boundary, transport framer), wired_rx/wired_tx (none beyond IX-1) | (a) For rates > 2.5 Gb/s run the host word clock at a fixed 312.5 MHz (rate-independent, payload counts absorb the difference) instead of line/8; (b) optional 8b/10b offload in the wired boundary: RX comma align + 10b→8b + K flag (9 bits/symbol), TX inverse with running disparity → host carries 0.9 × line: SATA2 2.70 (94 %), HBR 2.43 (84 %), 3.125 G 2.81 (98 %; ≤ 95 % with XAUI ‖I‖/‖A‖ or SATA ALIGN deletion and FPGA re-insertion). New register fields: `wire.linecode` (raw / 8b10b), idle-delete enable; both stopped-only. | WR-HL-LINECODE, HL-RATE |
| IX-3 | **Wired pad C for SATA Gen2 RL band to 2.4 GHz.** Gen2i ≥ 8 dB over 1.2–2.4 GHz ⇒ C_pad ≤ 1.15 pF effective (S7's 1.6 pF gives 5.7 dB at 2.4 GHz). HBR ≥ 10 dB to 1.35 GHz ⇒ ≤ 1.57 pF; XAUI ≥ 6 dB at 1.56 GHz ⇒ 1.6 pF passes (8.7 dB). | pads (+ wired_rx/wired_tx device C) | Tighten S7 to ≤ 1.15 pF effective up to 2.4 GHz, or a T-coil sized for 2.4 GHz; report RL 0.1–3 GHz on the COB chain (package-channel §6 already asks ≥ 8 dB at 2.5 GHz). | WR-SATA2-RL, WR-DPHBR-RL, WR-3G1-RL |
| IX-4 | **Low-rate mode below 0.48 Gb/s** documented as a rate mode, not only a fallback: TX via ring ÷2^k word/serializer clock; RX via FPGA oversampling of the full-rate slicer output (`wire_cdr_fpga_oversample`) or CDR at ring/2^k. | digital (clocktree divider), wired_rx (CDR at reduced rate) | Confirm divider range in clocktree; one CDR run at ring/2 (240 Mb/s). | WR-RATE-LOW |
| IX-5 | **LO phase settling for CS hopping.** The 50 µs warm-hop allocation (35 µs external settling) is a frequency number; CS needs the LO **phase-settled** to ≤ 2° from its final trajectory (residual transient ≤ 70 Hz at 80 µs) within T_FCS − 5 µs. Advertise T_FCS = 80 µs (stretch 50 µs). | timing (QVCO frequency loop, still open; `lo_ext`), board (B3/B19 synthesizer), digital (warm-hop sequencer) | On-chip PLL: loop BW / damping chosen for phase settling ≤ 75 µs from a 78 MHz hop; board: B3 synthesizer spec gains a phase-settling line (≤ 2° within 70 µs) — board-requirements owner to add. Sim: phase vs time after a hop. | RF-CS-HOP |
| IX-6 | **Timed, phase-continuous TX↔RX direction change** on the same channel ≤ 30 µs (T_IP2 ≤ 40 µs advertised; also serves 802.15.4 turnaround 192 µs). The LO, QVCO/lo_ext and converter sample clock stay running; only PA/LNA/mixer enables and the baseband path switch. Warm-hop today rejects a direction change as a hop, and the direction path is untimed. | rf_tx (driver/mute enable), rf_rx (LNA/mixer enable), timing (LO stays on), digital (direction sequencer, epoch handling without FIFO reset) | Settling sim of RX after TX-off (and TX after RX-off) with the real LO; ≤ 30 µs to within 1 dB / 2° | RF-CS-TIP, RF-154-TURN |
| IX-7 | **IQ phase / group-delay calibration path.** CS needs tone phase error ≤ 1° (IRR ≥ 35 dB after cal) held across 2402–2480 MHz, and the internal TX+RX round-trip delay known to ≤ 1 ns. The COB LO hybrid alone gives 6–10° I/Q error (D-LO-2 trims it coarsely). | rf_tx + rf_rx (loopback), timing (D-LO-2 I/Q trim), FPGA (per-bin cal) | A controllable TX→RX loopback with stable phase: either an on-chip attenuated coupler/switch (preferred) or the measured mute leakage (22–24 dB, RF-TX-MUTE) used deliberately, or a board coupler. Cal table per channel or ≤ 5 MHz bin. | RF-CS-IQ, RF-CS-PHASE, RF-CS-RANGE |
| IX-8 | **Deterministic TX-launch → RX-sample latency** ≤ 5 ns rms after calibration, readable, for RTT (and ±16 µs for Thread CSL). The action clock tick is 25.6 ns and hop/epoch FIFOs have unspecified latency. | digital (scheduler, converter FIFOs, epoch handshake), converters | Fixed pipeline latency per mode, a latency/status register, and an FPGA-visible sample-index timestamp at TX launch. | RF-CS-RTT, RF-THREAD-CSL |

Not new hardware: LE Coded (same GFSK PHY; only a 17 ms gain/DC/LO-hold requirement, RF-BLE-CODED-PKT), Zigbee and Matter (mapping only), CS antenna switching (N_AP = 1 on the first die; multi-antenna via an FPGA-driven external switch, no chip pin).

The plain SPICE-ratio method was rejected (−32 % tail vs the PDK ss_125C tables). Median cell-delay factor ss_032C/tt = **1.49** (p10 1.43, p90 1.64); ff_022C/tt 0.75. **Path level it is worse than the 1.54 derate**, because transitions and setup times grow more: CLOSE_R2 re-timed with the real ss_032C library gives WNS **−19.2 ns** (hpair_tx) vs −16.4 ns at 1.54×; sclk −10.2, ref −14.4, fc −11.8, wrx −13.3, ctw −7.3, ctdet −5.9. ff_022C hold: worst −0.001 ns (hpair_rx), all others ≥ +0.09. So do **not** remove pipeline stages; keep ≥ 10 % margin at the real ss library, which is now the default in `sta_signoff.py` (`PT_STA_LEGACY_DERATE=1` restores the derate corners; SDC `PT_LIB_CHARACTERIZED` disables the derate). | transport agent; flow | Next run: add the four libraries as STA corners. |
| D12 | **FF budget reply (T4):** my side = pt_mgmt 1689 + pt_memory 1586 + 2 × pt_freq_count 304 + PRBS 143 + 74 + clocktree 82 + pt_vreg_seq 25 + pt_interp2x 149 + pt_digital glue ≈ 75 = **≈ 4,430 FF** (yosys counts), inside your proposed 4,440. Accepted: transport ≤ 3,750, mine ≤ 4,440. Further reclaim if needed: the 2 × 32 × 24-bit capture/playback memories (1,536 FF) could become a gf180mcu SRAM macro or 16 entries. | transport agent | Please confirm. |
| D13 | **To the transport agent:** `sim/tb_digital_prbs.sv` no longer elaborates against the new `pt_digital` ports (h2d_quad); `make transceiver-pattern` fails there after PATTERN_PASS. | transport agent | Update the bench (port list). |
| D15 | **Macro decap (RA-12).** A thin-oxide filler cannot sit in the 5 V rows: KLayout DRC fails DV.9 (3.3 V and 5 V PMOS may not share the row NWELL) and DV.3/6/7/8 (the library DUALGATE overhangs 0.28 µm into neighbours). Built instead: `pt_decap50_{4,8,16,32,64}` (`verification/digital_hardening/decap/`, GDS/LEF/Liberty/SPICE), the library's own 5 V devices as 1 µm fingers sharing contact columns. KLayout DRC 0 abutted to library cells (only the well-tap distance rules, as for fillcap) and LVS match (method calibrated on fillcap_8). At 100 MHz, ss/32 °C/3.15 V: 0.67–0.71 fF/µm² for the 16/32-site cells vs 0.54 for fillcap (+24–31 %). The 4/8-site cells only match fillcap; the 64-site cell is 0.65 because of its single driver finger. Present fill ≈ 0.72 mm², 0.40 nF; with these cells ≈ **0.46 nF**. Upper bounds: thin oxide (4.0 fF/µm² gate) in dedicated decap-only row bands gives ≈ 1.8–2 fF/µm², so 2.5 nF needs ≈ 1.3 mm² of bands (+50 % macro area). MIM is 2 fF/µm². **The 2.5–4.7 nF cannot come from in-macro MOS fill.** | assembly/PDN | Put the bulk in the top-level MIM/decap pool. Digital can add `pt_decap50_16/32` to DECAP_CELL for the +15 %. |

## RX robustness review: hardware demands (2026-11-10)

Source: `scratch/reviews/rx-robustness-review.md`, risks RXR-01..12, requirements RF-RX-* / RF-COEX-PTA (interface-requirements.md §2.2), board B33–B37. Analysis only; no block circuits or RTL were edited.

| # | Demand | Owner(s) | Concrete ask |
|---|---|---|---|
| RXR-D1 | **Pre-filter gain range (RF-RX-MAXIN, RF-RX-AGC).** Today only gain_lo (−3 dB) and bypass (≈ −14 dB, NF ≈ 22 dB) act before the filter; the TIA clips at ≈ −34 dBm CW (estimate) against −10/−20 dBm max input. | rf_rx; digital (fields) | TIA feedback-R steps 20/10/5/2.5 kΩ (with matching C so the 20 MHz pole holds) and a 2-bit LNA stage-2 attenuation, ≥ 30 dB in ≤ 6 dB steps. New field `rxmix.tia_gain` (2 b) and `lna.att` (2 b), both on the fast gain command. Confirm with one compression/two-tone sim on promoted lna4_b + extracted mixers. |
| RXR-D2 | **Fast gain command (RF-RX-AGC).** opcode4 ("Atomic RF gain metadata") is specified but "the existing Receiver RTL does not implement opcode4". SPI-only gain is ≈ 3.8 µs/step. | digital (transport) | Implement opcode4 in the H2D receiver and drive LNA/TIA/PGA from it within ≤ 1 µs of FPGA decision; widen the argument to carry RXR-D1 bits (argument bits6:5 LNA are already reserved for 4 levels). |
| RXR-D3 | **Offset trim after the PGA (RF-RX-DCOC).** TIA DAC LSB 7–10 mV × PGA gain: residual 65 % FS at 42 dB. | rf_rx; digital | Add a 6-bit offset DAC at the PGA output / biquad input (LSB ≤ 1 mV there) per path, or 2 extra LSBs on the TIA DAC; field `bbfilter.offset_{i,q}` (direct). FPGA keeps a per-gain-code table. |
| RXR-D4 | **Overload detector (RF-RX-OVLD).** | rf_rx; digital | Two latched comparators per path at the TIA output, threshold ≈ 75 % of linear swing; `status.rx_ovld` (sticky, clear-on-read) and a flag in the D2H RF stream metadata. |
| RXR-D5 | **Sims to close the unverified rows** (≤ 3 sims). | rf_rx | (1) compression/IIP3/IIP2 + overload recovery at each gain state, (2) NF vs IF 10 kHz–10 MHz, (3) +10 dBm CW on the RF pad model powered/unpowered; plus AC PSRR of TIA+filter. |
| RXR-D6 | **Board items.** | board/spec | B33 T/R switch, B34 BAW coex filter footprint (populate in products with cellular/5 GHz), B35 LO synth phase-noise mask + one ±10 ppm TCXO for LO and converter/host clocks, B36 antenna TVS, B37 LDO-clean die supply. |
| RXR-D7 | **FPGA recipes.** | FPGA | Low-IF (1.5–3 MHz LO offset) for BLE/15.4/LoRa; AGC using ADC power + RXR-D4 flag; DC tracking; CFO ±250 kHz + SFO tracking; 3-wire PTA (RF-COEX-PTA). |

| PD19 | **Wired pad C, IX-3 and termination trim (S7, IX-3, PKG-W1a).** The gate-coupled shunt does **not** change pad C (it sits behind the steering diodes): wirepad 2.27 → 2.27 pF off, txpad 2.76 → 2.76 pF off at 1.25 GHz (`pd13-cap-*.json`). Breakdown at 1.25 GHz (tt, extracted, one contributor removed at a time): **wirepad** termination branch 0.63 (off state; when terminated it is shunted: 1.74 pF total), interconnect C on PAD 0.54, USB FS driver branch 0.48, MOS directly on the pad (HS switches, bypass) 0.31, steering diodes 0.23 pF; **txpad** driver/cascode/detect devices on the pad 1.49, termination 0.50, interconnect 0.50, diodes 0.23 pF. Die-level differential RL with the termination on (`pd13-rl-*.json`, no package): **wirepad 8.4–9.9 dB at 1.25 GHz, 4.7–5.6 dB at 2.4 GHz** (1.75–1.82 pF/leg); **txpad 6.8–8.7 / 4.6–6.1 dB** (1.7–2.8 pF/leg). IX-3 needs ≥ 8 dB to 2.4 GHz ⇒ ≤ 1.15 pF. **This is not a GF180 limit:** protection + bond pad is ≈ 0.4 pF (diodes 0.23 + pad metal ≈ 0.15), i.e. ≈ 13 dB at 2.4 GHz is reachable on a pad that carries only protection, the termination and a receiver gate. What blocks it is the shared-pad architecture (USB FS/HS drivers and TMDS-class TX devices on the same pins). Achievable on the present cells with the GC shunt: 1.74 pF terminated (RX), 2.38 pF (TX). **Termination trim (designed, schematic-on-extraction, `pd13-termtrim2c.json`):** the drawn RX termination is switch-dominated (46/53/40 Ω tt/ss/ff at VCM 0, up to 73 Ω at ss/VCM 1 V) and a trim of that switch cannot reach 40–60 Ω at every corner (`pd13-termtrim.json`: ss/VCM 1 V never below 55 Ω, ff never above 50 Ω). Replacement per leg: **25 identical poly-dominated units** (ppolyf_u 2 × 3.05 µm + nfet_03v3 10/0.28 µm to TERM_VCM), 10 base units on with TERM_ENABLE + 15 trim units on per `TERM_TRIM[3:0]` (binary 1/2/4/8). Result over tt27/ss22/ss32/ff22/ff32 × VCM 0/0.5/1.0 V: code 0 ≥ 65.1 Ω, code 15 ≤ 40.2 Ω (worst ss/32 °C/VCM 1 V), steps 2.5–3.5 Ω in the 40–60 Ω window; nominal 50 Ω at code ≈ 5 (tt). Off-state pad C −0.34 pF (1.93 vs 2.27 pF), terminated C +0.02 pF (1.76 pF). VCM above ≈ 1.2 V makes any NMOS-switch termination non-linear (ss: > 150 Ω at VCM 1.65 V); the trim is specified for TERM_VCM ≤ 1.0 V. | pads, wired RX/USB, wired TX, digital, assembly | **Wired RX/USB + assembly (architectural, demanded by IX-3):** give SATA/PCIe RX its own low-C pad pair (protection + segmented termination + CTLE gate only; USB FS/HS stay on the shared wirepad), or accept a T-coil per leg sized for 2.4 GHz (≈ 4.5 nH total per leg for 1.8 pF at 50 Ω; ≈ 0.04 mm² per leg in metal5). Pads will lay out whichever is chosen. **Wired TX:** same question for the TX pins (driver C 1.49 pF is the TX's own). **Digital:** `termination.config.trim[3:0]` (4 static bits per wired RX pad pair, reset code 5); termination enable unchanged. **Pads:** lay out the segmented termination in `receive_pad_layout.py` (next). |

Known settling: bbfilter TX→RX direction 0.15 µs (extracted); rfdriver cascode gates are static (6 pF decaps) and the pads stay DC-fed by the board, so no pad-DC transient; island switches must NOT toggle (isw_rf 1–4.6 µs ramp plus the 36–49 mA RF_TX pad-clamp inrush of PD6). Remaining sim: LNA/mixer enable → bb settling ≤ 30 µs to 1 dB/2° (not run). IX-7: **no new on-chip RF coupler** (a die-crossing TX→RX path is an RX isolation risk); use the measured TX-mute leakage (22–24 dB) with the LNA on, or the board coupler, for RF loopback, and the existing bbfilter TX-direction paths for baseband delay; per-channel cal in the FPGA. IX-8: converter latencies are deterministic: DAC output follows the code on the 2nd clock edge (verified in the 80 MS/s test: coherent FFT), ADC conversion ≤ 30 ns inside its clock slot; the analog filter group delay is corner/qt-dependent and must be calibrated through the IX-7 loopback. | digital (sequencer, latency register), timing (LO), FPGA | Digital: direction change without island toggling or FIFO reset; a latency/status register. |
| RC-24 | **RXR-D1..D5 (RX robustness), first pass — designs in scratch, deciding sims run; nothing promoted.** Scratch variant `scratch/rfconv-owner/rxr/netlists_rxr.py`: rxmix `tia_gain[1:0]` (TIA feedback 20/10/5/2.5 kΩ with C scaled ×1/2/4/8 via TGs), overload detector (two 5T comparators per path at the TIA outputs vs ≈ VCM + 0.5 V, OR → SR latch, `ovl_clr`/`ovl`), LNA `att[1:0]` (shunts across the stage-1 drains). Schematic chain, ideal match, CW at LO+5 MHz, TIA output: max gain 40.2 dB small-signal, −0.6 dB at −30 dBm, −10 dB at −20 dBm (P1dB ≈ −28 dBm, slightly better than the −34 dBm review estimate). **But the steps are weak:** tia_gain 3 gives −8.2 dB (not −18: the passive mixer + LNA source impedance is comparable to Rtia), att 3 gives −2.3 dB (the 2.4 GHz stage-1 node is capacitive), and in the lowest state the LNA itself compresses near −25 dBm (14 dB gain at −10 dBm, 10 dB compressed). **Conclusion: RXR-D1 needs attenuation ahead of stage 1** (switched input attenuator / divider across the gates, or the bypass path + steps), the TIA steps then add ≈ 8 dB; ≥ 30 dB in ≤ 6 dB steps is not met by the requested topology. D3 (post-PGA offset DAC): not implemented; sizing note: a 6-bit DAC with ≤ 1 mV LSB covers only ±31 mV while the TIA-DAC residual at 42 dB is ±0.6 V, so it needs 2 extra TIA-DAC LSBs **and** ≥ 8 bits after the PGA. D5 NF-vs-IF, IIP2/IIP3, +10 dBm survival and PSRR: not run this pass. | rf_rx (owner, open), digital (fields when promoted) | **Digital, reserve (not yet in silicon):** `rxmix_{i,q}.tia_gain[1:0]` reset 0, `lna.att[1:0]` reset 0, `rxmix_{i,q}.ofs` widened to 7 bits, `bbfilter_{i,q}.offset[7:0]`, `status.rx_ovld_{i,q}` (sticky, clear-on-read via `ovl_clr`). All on the fast gain command (RXR-D2). |
